Measure: Prediction error, interval coverage and decision sensitivity; outcome effects need later observation.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Source claim locator ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S017-L1</p></li><li><p>S017-L2</p></li><li><p>S018-L1</p></li><li><p>S018-L2</p></li><li><p>S018-L3</p></li><li><p>S027-L1</p></li><li><p>S027-L2</p></li><li><p>S027-L3</p></li><li><p>S056-L1</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Source role note</dt><dd><p>Sources ground the bounded mechanism and its criticism. They do not assert the exact analytical wording or validate this full composition; sources on a rejected row support its examination, not advocacy of the universal claim.</p></dd><dt>Neutral audit questions</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Is the stated trigger present, and what real decision or affected outcome makes this candidate relevant?</p></li><li><p>What existing mechanism, actor and evidence establish the stated postcondition, or show that a simpler alternative or omission is sufficient?</p></li><li><p>How are the candidate-specific risks handled: Precisely wrong forecasts, warm-up mismatch, unrepresented work, narrow intervals with poor coverage and overfitting.</p></li><li><p>What observation would warrant revision or an evidence-insufficient judgement? Would the preferred redesign change under plausible misspecification of demand, resources or unlogged work?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Allowed audit outcomes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>ALREADY_ADDRESSED</p></li><li><p>INAPPLICABLE</p></li><li><p>SIMPLER_MECHANISM_SUFFICIENT</p></li><li><p>CONDITIONAL_IMPROVEMENT_OPPORTUNITY</p></li><li><p>EVIDENCE_INSUFFICIENT</p></li><li><p>UNRESOLVED_RESEARCH_QUESTION</p></li><li><p>NO_UNCONDITIONAL_OBLIGATION</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Requalification</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current examination</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED_AT_DECLARED_SCOPE</p></dd><dt>Predicate</dt><dd><p>Simulation results are conditional on model, state and intervention stability, with uncertainty appropriate to the decision.</p></dd><dt>Countercontrol</dt><dd><p>Good retrospective fit may fail after a policy changes worker behaviour; a reversible trial may be more informative.</p></dd><dt>New evidence source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S056</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Interpretation provenance</dt><dd><p>Current author analysis; native conditional prose preserved; not target assessment</p></dd><dt>Record ref</dt><dd><p>REQUALIFICATION.json#/candidate_reexamination/20</p></dd><dt>Review state</dt><dd><p>NOT_PERFORMED</p></dd></dl></dd></dl></dd><dt>Current native locator</dt><dd><dl><dt>Path</dt><dd><p>EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json</p></dd><dt>Bytes</dt><dd><p>1122330</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>99fa73496fc8d720c8b5f61f6ec85837b70287786ebde4422a1ea0bf0a41b60e</p></dd><dt>Json pointer</dt><dd><p>/properties/20</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Historical profile locator</dt><dd><dl><dt>Path</dt><dd><p>EVOLVED_BWP_CONSTITUENT_PROPERTY_REGISTER.json</p></dd><dt>Json pointer</dt><dd><p>/properties/20</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Current interpretation policy</dt><dd><p>The complete exact qualified native record controls; these fields are source projections. <a id="EBPM-EBPM-022"></a>
## EBPM:EBPM-022

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-003.md#EBPM-EBPM-022">EBPM:EBPM-022</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-022</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Causal restraint in intervention selection</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B4</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S019</p></li><li><p>S002</p></li><li><p>S027</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. State the proposed mechanism, plausible confounding and comparison; use stronger designs or bounded trials when an observational association could recommend the wrong intervention.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A variant, actor or activity is blamed for an outcome, or a predicted association is used to choose a change.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A modest reversible trial with explicit observations can be more informative than a complex causal model based on weak assumptions.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>A defined intervention and outcome; relevant case mix; an honest account of what identifies causation and what remains assumed.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/5d.md#r-5d04b8c45668df58c383463c2e8aac491910e559048f5e86aac3dcd896e5dd1f">Complete shared assumptions · 5d04b8c45668</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analysts can identify evidence limits; authorised owners decide on precaution, experimentation or non-intervention.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Explain why the proposed comparison distinguishes the causal story from plausible rivals.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/59.md#r-59f96a893af67cc7cb5299584ff4292b73e0d34560832d2c2676f98ef4b10861">Complete shared consumer · 59f96a893af6</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The decision states whether evidence is descriptive, predictive or causally identifying, with material alternative explanations visible.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Selection bias, conditioning on colliders, hidden common causes and reverse causation.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Selection bias, conditioning on colliders, hidden common causes and reverse causation.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S019’s causal models have explicit assumptions; S031 and S040 are associational. Compare fewer handoffs with the competence and concentration-of-authority costs of task combination.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Cases repeatedly lose context or stall between fragmented responsibilities.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A shared case note and named coordinator can be enough; do not require one person to perform every specialist task.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Competence, time, access to information and a legitimate division between coordination and decision powers.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/a1.md#r-a14daf4734ded5bff12a60ecfdd2dfa8f52cd1fc9e9359ed42083d6ca312df2c">Complete shared assumptions · a14daf4734de</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>A coordinator may organise contributions without acquiring every professional or financial approval power.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Specialists and coordinators communicate decisions, unresolved issues and recipient needs.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><dl><dt>Decision</dt><dd><p>Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. Cases repeatedly lose context or stall between fragmented responsibilities.</p></dd><dt>Consumer and authority</dt><dd><p>A coordinator may organise contributions without acquiring every professional or financial approval power.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>Case continuity improves while required specialist/control functions remain effective.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A single overloaded coordinator becomes a bottleneck; accountability is concentrated without competence.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>A single overloaded coordinator becomes a bottleneck; accountability is concentrated without competence.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S016 explicitly discusses training and segregation-of-duties constraints; S026 cautions against formalism that degrades professional judgement.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S001">EBPM:S001</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-002.md#EBPM-S015">EBPM:S015</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-002.md#EBPM-S016">EBPM:S016</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S026">EBPM:S026</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R015</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R016</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R019</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R052</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R053</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A shared case note and named coordinator can be enough; do not require one person to perform every specialist task.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent.
